# Humanoid Families 11-12 — Ratfolk (5) + Naga (2, ex-snakefolk), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 48. ENY_RATFOLK_SNITCH — Ratfolk Snitch
Whisker-twitching scouts infest granary walls where spilled grain sweetens every dark breath. Their bands breed quickly inside tiled culverts the empire built and successor towns forgot. They trade stolen manifests for cheese-rinds and sheltered nesting corners. Merchants secretly pay them to learn rivals’ hidden stores. They fear seasoned mousers far more than sleepy human watchmen. In battle they dart between legs with short knives, overwhelming isolated victims through numbers. They scatter screaming the moment their chieftains fall. Their musky droppings betray every regular route.
Combat: 3/1/5/1, always_physical.
Appearance: child-sized rat-headed skulker with short knife and grain-sack, whiskers trembling, tail balancing behind human knees. Patchy fur, red eyes. States: wall-niche crouch / darting pack-stab / tail-yanked stagger / piled whimpering surrender. Variants: 2M young squeaker / grey whisperer; 2F breeding dam / quick sister.

## 49. ENY_RATFOLK_BLADE — Ratfolk Blade
Scarred tunnel fighters guard nesting warrens with rusted swords longer than themselves. Their bands raid cellars along streets the night watch patrols thinly. They learned pack tactics watching stray dogs surround butchers’ boys. Butchers hang their pelts on doors as warnings. They respect only the biggest teeth. In battle they surge with rusted blades, trading wounds without hesitation. They break when fire appears. Their nests stink of tallow and fear.
Combat: 4/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: battle-scarred rat warrior with rusted sword and sewn-hide armor, standing waist-high to humans, tail lashing. One ear torn. States: tunnel-mouth guard / surging blade-swarm / fire-panic scatter stagger / kneeling with dropped sword. Variants: 2M young fighter / scarred guard; 2F hard dam-fighter / older brood-guard.

## 50. ENY_RATFOLK_PLAGUE — Ratfolk Plague Carrier (disease as mundane filth, no new magic)
Filth-caked carriers spread spoilage through wells they bathe in deliberately. Their bands weaponize rot where old sewers collapsed. They understand contagion through observed custom, never through sorcery. Villagers burn their bedding on discovery. They trade antidotes they distrust themselves. In battle they hurl dung-pots then stab with filthy daggers, weakening foes through disgust. They wear cloth masks against their own stench. Boiled water defeats them.
Combat: 3/1/4/2, cycle (filth-then-stab, mundane).
Appearance: pox-marked carrier with dung-pot bandolier and stained mask, fur matted, flies circling human-height clouds. Oozing sores. States: pot-ready hover / dung-toss dagger rush / coughing stagger / slumped over broken pots. Variants: 2M young mixer / pocked master; 2F sharp mixer / older dam.

## 51. ENY_RATFOLK_ENGINEER — Ratfolk Engineer
Clever wire-twisters build deadfall traps and cranked dart-throwers from stolen bell parts. Their devices mimic old artillery in miniature without gunpowder sorcery. Millers pay them to kill competitors’ wheels. They love clicking mechanisms above all food. In battle they trigger spring-snares then swarm with wrenches. Their machines jam constantly. Oil and spare wire sustain them. Smashed workshops end them.
Combat: 3/1/5/2, always_physical.
Appearance: gear-laden tinkerer with wrench-club and wire coils, goggles of bottle-glass, standing knee-high to humans. Grease-smeared whiskers. States: device-crouch calibration / snare-spring wrench rush / jammed-device stagger / seated among broken gears. Variants: 2M young twister / grey inventor; 2F quick assembler / older keeper.

## 52. ENY_RATFOLK_BROODMOTHER — Ratfolk Broodmother (band-level only)
Vast breeding mothers anchor warrens, birthing litters that replenish every lost raid. Their hunger dictates every foray into granaries and kitchens. Bands starve without their steady flow. They remember no history beyond full bellies. In battle they defend nesting chambers with crushing bulk and teeth, never pursuing beyond. Their death dissolves warrens into quarreling packs. Rival mothers absorb survivors. Warmth and grain sustain them.
Combat: 5/1/2/4, always_physical.
Appearance: enormous bloated mother with crushing paws and swollen belly, filling tunnel mouths, dwarfs human children. Milk-heavy, blind with protectiveness. States: nest-blocking bulk / crushing bite sweep / pup-loss frenzy stagger / collapsed nursing surrender. Variants: 4F young mother / prime dam / grey dam / barren aunt (female-led reproduction, males 0 with reason: breeding hierarchy).

## 53. ENY_NAGA_CONSTRICTOR — Naga Constrictor (ex-snakefolk)
Heavy-bodied constrictor bands coil in steaming marsh reeds beyond the last farm ditches. Their scaled torsos rise human-high above muscular tails that crush wild pigs. They learned ambush patience before successor roads existed. Fisherfolk trade smoked eels for safe passage through their waters. They worship no gods, only warm stones. In battle they grapple and squeeze lone victims, avoiding formed spear lines. They release dead prey slowly. Their shed skins mark territories.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, cycle (coil-squeeze behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: human-torsoed constrictor with green-brown scales and crushing tail, rearing taller than humans, tongue flickering. Broad shoulders. States: reed-coil ambush / grapple-squeeze / spear-prodded release stagger / uncoiled gasping surrender. Variants: 2M young coil / scarred crusher; 2F brooding dam / older shedder.

## 54. ENY_NAGA_AMBUSHER — Naga Ambusher
Smaller mottled ambushers haunt forest-floor litter where sun breaks in speckles. Their bands coordinate silent surrounds with tail-taps. They take deer and unwary travelers alike. Charcoal-burners dread their leaf-rustle. They collect warm stones. In battle they spring from cover with short spears, constricting stragglers. They abandon hunts against fire. Their nests hold clutch-eggs.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, cycle (spring-grapple behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: mottled small constrictor with short spear and leaf-litter cloak, lower than humans when coiled, eyes amber. Quick tongue. States: litter-hidden coil / springing spear-grapple / fire-driven release stagger / coiled shielding eggs surrender. Variants: 2M young striker / grey waiter; 2F nesting dam / older hunter.

# Humanoid Families 13-15 — Mermaids (5, F-only) + Sphinxes (5, F-only, ex-bird-maidens) + Sirens (2, F-only, ex-half-bird-half-fish), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free. All-female bands with reason. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 55. ENY_MERMAID_REEF — Reef Mermaid
Pale reef mermaids haunt sheltered coastal shallows where braided channels meet the sea. Their bands comb tide-pools for crabs and drowned sailors’ coins. They sing wordless harmonies that carry across evening water. Fisherfolk trade hooks for warnings about coming storms. They fear deep currents and iron nets equally. In battle they drag waders down with nets and coral knives, avoiding dry land entirely. They release catches for shiny ransoms. Their reef gardens mark territories.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: silver-tailed maiden with coral knife and fishing net, torso human-like above shimmering scales, eyes dark and wide. Hair seaweed-long. States: shallows-song lure / net-drag pull / beached flop stagger / submerged pleading surrender. Variants: 4F young singer / scarred diver / grey chorister / old net-keeper.

## 56. ENY_MERMAID_SURF — Surf Mermaid
Strong surf mermaids ride breakers beyond reef lines, bolder than reef kin. Their bands salvage wrecks after storms. They understand tides better than any pilot. Coastal villagers leave wreck shares on beaches. They collect glass and coins. In battle they overturn skiffs with wave-timed rushes, spearing swimmers. They cannot pursue inland. Their salvage caves glitter.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: muscular surf maiden with salvage spear and rope harness, tail powerful, shoulders broad from swimming. Foam in hair. States: breaker-riding approach / skiff-overturn spear / stranded gasp stagger / wave-washed retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young rider / scarred salvor / grey pilot / old hoarder.

## 57. ENY_MERMAID_DEEP — Deep Mermaid
Blind deep mermaids rise only on moonless nights from trenches beyond sight. Their bands follow whale falls. They navigate by pressure changes and cold currents. Sailors call them omens. They trade abyssal pearls for surface news. They cannot endure sunlight. In battle they envelop boats in ink-dark water, pulling with many arms. They vanish at dawn.
Combat: 5/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: pale blind maiden with enlarged eyes and ink-glands, many-ringed arms, bioluminescent markings. Movements slow. States: dark-water rise / enveloping pull / sun-struck recoil stagger / sinking surrender. Variants: 4F young riser / scarred diver / grey oracle / old trench-keeper.

## 58. ENY_MERMAID_RIVER — River Mermaid
Freshwater river mermaids nest in delta reeds where currents slow. Their bands guide lost boats for tolls of bread. They know every sandbar shift. Barge crews sing back to them. They fear dams and mill-wheels. In battle they tangle poles and drag swimmers to shallows, never killing outright if ransomed. They remember kind boatmen. Their reed bowers line banks.
Combat: 3/1/4/3, always_physical.
Appearance: green-scaled river maiden with reed-woven sash and pole-hook, fins small, smile quick. Mud-flecked. States: reed-song guide / pole-tangle drag / dammed-strand flop stagger / bower retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young guide / scarred toll-keeper / grey memory / old bower-mother.

## 59. ENY_MERMAID_STORM — Storm Matron (band-level only)
Storm matrons lead mermaid bands, reading weather in gull flight and water taste. Their counsel decides migrations. They shelter bands in caves during gales. Sailors beg their forecasts. They demand wreck-rights as price. In battle they coordinate multi-wave rushes with conch calls, shielding young behind. Their fall scatters bands to rival matrons. Their conchs are heirlooms.
Combat: 4/1/5/5, always_physical.
Appearance: imposing storm maiden with conch-horn and storm-scarred tail, presence commanding, older and larger. White hair. States: conch-call command / wave-rush coordination / horn-loss stagger / cave-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young herald / prime matron / grey storm-reader / old conch-keeper.

## 60. ENY_SPHINX_CLIFF — Cliff Sphinx (ex-bird-maiden)
High cliff sphinxes nest on ledges where sea winds defeat every climbing predator. Their bands drop feathers as boundary warnings. They sing dawn choruses that shepherds use as clocks. Goatherds trade wool for fallen arrowheads. They fear cave snakes and lowland nets. In battle they drop stones from height then rake with talons, never landing among spears. They ransom seized goats for shiny wire. Their guano streaks mark territories for miles.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, cycle (chorus-allure behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: feather-winged maiden with taloned feet and stone-pile, arms human, wings broad, eyes fierce. Wind-tossed hair. States: ledge-song hover / stone-drop talon rake / grounded wing-drag stagger / cliff-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young fledgling / scarred dropper / grey chorister / old ledge-keeper.

## 61. ENY_SPHINX_FOREST — Forest Sphinx
Canopy sphinxes haunt tall timber where light falls in broken shafts. Their bands follow boar herds to steal offal. They mimic hawk cries to scatter hunters. Charcoal-burners leave fat scraps beneath roosts. They collect bright string. In battle they entangle with dropped nets then dive with short spears, avoiding sustained melee. They abandon hunts against fire. Their roosts hold stolen spoons.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, cycle (mimic-cry behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: brown-winged maiden with net-coils and short spear, camouflaged plumage, quiet eyes. Leaf-litter scented. States: canopy-hidden perch / net-drop spear dive / net-tangled stagger / roost-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young flutter / scarred hunter / grey mimic / old roost-mother.

## 62. ENY_SPHINX_MARSH — Marsh Sphinx
Wading marsh sphinxes stalk reed-beds on stilt-long legs. Their bands spear eels at dusk. They trade feathers for reed-baskets. Fisherfolk imitate their calls. They fear drainage crews. In battle they impale waders with long bills turned to spears, retreating over water. They ransom boats for fish. Their stilt-nests flood yearly.
Combat: 3/1/4/2, cycle (wading-lure behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: stilt-legged wading maiden with bill-spear and basket-harness, wings short, legs impossibly long. Reed-crowned. States: reed-stalk wade / bill-spear jab / grounded leg-fold stagger / water-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young wader / scarred eeler / grey caller / old nest-keeper.

## 63. ENY_SPHINX_STORM — Storm Sphinx
Broad-winged storm sphinxes ride gales above high plateaus. Their bands follow thunderheads for updrafts. They read weather in cloud color. Herders watch their flight for warnings. They scorn lowland sisters. In battle they dive through gales with slate shards, scattering lines. They cannot fly in still air. Their cliff eyries hold storm-glass.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, cycle (gale-call behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: broad-winged high maiden with slate-shard satchel, powerful chest, storm-grey plumage. Scarred beak. States: gale-riding circle / dive-shard scatter / becalmed flop stagger / eyrie-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young glider / scarred diver / grey reader / old eyrie-keeper.

## 64. ENY_SPHINX_NIGHT — Night Matron (band-level only)
Night matrons lead sphinx flocks, singing routes across dark miles. Their counsel chooses migrations. They shelter flocks in caves during storms. Travelers follow their songs to safety. They demand tribute in songbirds’ eggs. In battle they coordinate moonlit dives with drum-calls, shielding fledglings behind. Their fall scatters flocks to rival matrons. Their drums are stretched hide.
Combat: 4/1/5/5, cycle (night-song behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: imposing night maiden with drum and moon-pale wings, presence commanding, larger and older. Silvered feathers. States: drum-call command / coordinated moon-dive / drum-loss stagger / cave-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young herald / prime matron / grey night-reader / old drum-keeper.

## 65. ENY_SIREN_SHORE — Shore Siren (half-bird-half-fish)
Gull-winged shore sirens nest upon wave-cut ledges where tall cliffs drop into foaming tide-pools. Their small bands sing layered evening harmonies that drift across glass-calm water for miles. Fisherfolk plug their ears with wax before rowing past the point. They gather wreck coins and bright glass in shore gardens. They dread iron nets and deep winter gales equally. In battle they lure wading victims close with compelling song, then drag them down with taloned hands. They avoid armored boats and massed archers entirely. They ransom captured sailors for fresh water and bread.
Combat: 4/1/5/2, cycle (compelling-song behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: half-feathered half-scaled maiden with gull wings and fish tail folding into legs ashore, voice honeyed, eyes sea-green. Salt-crusted hair. States: ledge-song lure / talon-drag pull / beached gasp stagger / sea-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young singer / scarred wrecker / grey harmonist / old shore-keeper.

## 66. ENY_SIREN_DEEP — Deep Siren
Eel-tailed deep sirens rise from cold trenches on moonless nights following whale migrations. Their songs vibrate through hulls rather than air. Sailors call them keel-whisperers. They trade abyssal shells for news of wars. They cannot endure sunlight or warm shallows. In battle they surround hulls with harmonized pressure waves, stunning swimmers before pulling. They vanish before dawn. Their trenches remain unmapped by any pilot.
Combat: 5/1/5/2, cycle (hull-hum behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: large eel-tailed siren with vestigial wings and abyssal markings, voice felt in bones, size greater than humans. Blind-pale eyes. States: hull-circling hum / pressure-wave stun pull / sun-struck recoil stagger / trench-sink surrender. Variants: 4F young riser / scarred hummer / grey oracle / old trench-mother.

## 67. ENY_ONI_BRUTE — Oni Brute
Iron-club oni brutes haunt mountain passes where thunderheads gather each summer afternoon. Their red-skinned frames tower over human travelers, horned foreheads scraping low branches. They learned gate-smashing serving long-vanished warlords. Villagers leave rice wine at boundary shrines to deflect them. They collect ironware. They fear fast rivers. In battle they sweep roads with studded clubs, scattering escorts. They challenge champions to single bouts.
Combat: 7/1/2/3, always_physical.

States: pass-blocking stance / club-sweep road-clear / river-fear stagger / seated with offered wine surrender. Variants: 2M young horn / scarred crusher; 2F hard horn-mother / older keeper.

## 68. ENY_ONI_SMASHER — Oni Smasher
Blue-skinned smashers dwell in quarry wastes, breaking stone for sport and road-tolls. Their bands compete in boulder-throwing contests. They understand masonry better than soldiers. Quarry crews bribe them with cheap wine. They hate bells. In battle they hurl rubble then close with fists, slow but unstoppable. They spare children. Their waste-piles grow yearly.
Combat: 7/1/2/2, always_physical.
Appearance: massive blue-skinned breaker with rubble-satchel and bare fists, horns short, quarry dust ingrained. States: waste-stance heft / rubble-hail fist rush / bell-rung stagger / seated among rubble surrender. Variants: 2M young thrower / grey breaker; 2F hard smasher / older matron.

## 69. ENY_KAPPA_RIVER — River Kappa
Bowl-headed river imps lurk in deep pools where currents slow beneath overhanging willows. Their bands dam side-channels with stones to trap fish. They challenge travelers to sumo bouts for safe passage. Fisherfolk bow politely to spill their head-water. They love cucumbers above all tribute. They fear iron nets. In battle they drag waders down with webbed claws, grappling toward deep water. They release victims who bow deeply.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: child-sized turtle-like imp with water-bowl crown and webbed claws, shell-backed, beak-mouthed beside human knees. Wet stink. States: pool-lurk bow / drag-down grapple / spilled-bowl weaken stagger / deeply bowing surrender. Variants: 2M young wrestler / scarred elder; 2F hard wife / older keeper.

## 70. ENY_KAPPA_MARSH — Marsh Kappa
Mud-caked marsh kappas inhabit reed mazes where channels braid and vanish. Their bands steal melons and tools from farm edges. They memorize every hidden plank. Farmers curse their thefts nightly. They return favors strictly. In battle they ambush from mud with short spears, retreating into water. They trade stolen goods for cucumbers. Their mud-nests hide clutches.
Combat: 3/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: mud-armored small imp with short spear and melon-sack, bowl mossy, eyes wary. Squelching steps. States: mud-hidden ambush / spear-jab retreat / dried-bowl stagger / offering-melon surrender. Variants: 2M young thief / grey plotter; 2F quick thief / older nester.

# Humanoid Family 18 — Monkey-folk (5, high-count; no monkey Beasts), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 71. ENY_MONKEY_SCOUT — Monkey Scout
Long-tailed scouts swing swiftly through tangled canopy highways arching above dim forest trails. Their noisy bands can strip ripening orchards completely within frantic minutes. They warn distant troops with sharp barking calls echoing across valleys. Orchard keepers hire slingers specifically to deter their relentless raids. They covet shiny bottlecaps and cracked mirrors above even food. In battle they hurl heavy branches downward before mobbing victims with sticks. They flee whistling immediately at the first baying of dogs. Their habitual trails span many miles of connected branches.
Combat: 3/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: long-tailed agile climber carrying a stick-club and bottlecap necklace, fur brown-grey, sized like a human child. Prehensile tail curling around branches constantly. States: canopy-swing scouting posture / branch-hail mobbing rush / dog-panic scattering stagger / treetop chattering surrender. Variants: 2M young swinger / grey caller; 2F quick mother / older watcher.

## 72. ENY_MONKEY_ARCHER — Monkey Archer
Short-bow monkey troops loose surprisingly disciplined volleys from wildly swaying high branches. Their chattering bands practice daily upon painted gourds hung from neighboring limbs. They guard prized fruit territories fiercely against all neighboring troops. Rival troops wage noisy seasonal wars over contested boundary trees. They fletch their arrows with bright shed feathers gathered each molt. In battle they volley downwards before relocating screaming through canopy, avoiding melee entirely. Their bowstrings rot quickly during prolonged rainy seasons. Their shattered gourd-targets litter every established camp clearing.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: bow-carrying monkey soldier with gourd-quiver and feather armbands, balanced by a curling tail, eyes keen and quick. Compact disciplined posture. States: branch-aim poised crouch / chattering volley release / string-snap fumbling stagger / branch-retreat chattering surrender. Variants: 2M young bow / grey marksman; 2F keen archer / older fletcher.

## 73. ENY_MONKEY_CHIEF — Monkey Chief (band-level only)
Grizzled chiefs hold quarrelsome troops together through grooming favors and thunderous dominance displays. Their echoing screams settle daily disputes over food and sleeping branches. They personally lead every major orchard raid from the front. They remember fat fruit years across many long decades. In battle they direct screeching mass charges while flourishing iron-bound staffs, dueling rival chiefs mid-brawl. Their sudden fall splits troops into warring splinters instantly. Their favorite high perches overlook entire home ranges widely. Advancing age greys their heavy muzzles white.
Combat: 4/1/4/4, always_physical.
Appearance: massive grizzled leader carrying an iron-bound staff with scarred muzzle, bulk dwarfing troop-mates, presence overwhelmingly loud. Grey mane bristling. States: perch-display roaring stance / staff-led charging advance / rival-wound reeling stagger / deposed grooming surrender. Variants: 2M young challenger / scarred holder; 2F hard matron / older aunt.

## 74. ENY_MONKEY_THIEF — Monkey Thief
Pouch-cheeked thieves slip silently into human camps to lift warm bread and shining tools. Their furtive bands trade stolen goods onward through trained magpies. They memorize every patrol gap within only a few nights. Camp cooks hang food higher each passing week. They will trade almost anything for fermented wine. In battle they snatch pouches and flee immediately, stabbing desperately only when fully cornered. Their bulging cheek-pouches betray every single theft. Barking dogs deter them far more effectively than spears.
Combat: 2/1/5/1, always_physical.
Appearance: bulging-cheeked sneak carrying a thief-sack and small knife, tail curled tight for silence, eyes darting constantly. Hunched furtive posture. States: camp-slipping sneaking crouch / snatch-and-flee dashing rush / cornered knife-flailing stagger / screeching empty-pouch surrender. Variants: 2M young sneak / grey lifter; 2F quick mother / older fence.

## 75. ENY_MONKEY_SINGER — Monkey Singer
Howling singers greet every dawn with tremendous choruses carrying clearly for many miles. Their resonant bands effectively map vast territories through answering songs. They mourn their dead with night-long wailing ceremonies. Lonely travelers use their punctual choruses as reliable forest clocks. They hate unnatural silence far more than any predator. In battle they deafen foes with coordinated howling before pelting them with hard fruit, rallying troops simultaneously. Their sudden loss silences entire bands mournfully. Their bright throat-sacs swell hugely while singing loudly.
Combat: 2/1/4/4, always_physical.
Appearance: big-throated howler carrying a fruit-satchel with scarred voice-box, throat-sac vivid red, posture upright and proud. Resonant chest. States: dawn-chorus upright stance / deafening howl fruit-pelting assault / throat-seized coughing stagger / silent crouching surrender. Variants: 2M young howler / grey dawn-caller; 2F loud mother / older mourner.

# Humanoid Family 19 — Fox-maidens (5, high-count, female-only; distinct from Beast cinder foxes animals; no fox Fiends), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 76. ENY_FOXMAIDEN_RUST — Rust Maiden
Russet-furred fox maidens den secretly among dense autumn bracken where tall farm hedges grow thick. Their close bands raise wriggling cubs communally while hunting rabbits across moonlit fields. They trade gathered forest herbs for precious iron needles. Farmers blame them loudly for every missing chicken. They adore spoken riddles above silver. In battle they harry foes with short knives, circling swiftly on silent paws. They vanish instantly into familiar bracken runs. Their hidden earths shift quietly each season.
Combat: 4/1/5/2, cycle (riddle-allure behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: russet-furred vixen-woman with short knife and herb-pouch, ears pricked, tail sweeping, human-graceful and low. Amber eyes. States: bracken-crouch listening / circling knife-harass / tail-seized stagger / earth-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young vixen / scarred hunter / grey storyteller / old earth-mother.

## 77. ENY_FOXMAIDEN_SNOW — Snow Maiden
White-furred snow maidens haunt windswept high plateau drifts where winter never fully releases its grip. Their silent bands follow migrating ptarmigan flocks across white expanses. They read approaching blizzards accurately in subtle wind shifts. Mountain herders leave bowls of milk at boundary cairns. They collect rare blue glass from wrecked caravans. In battle they ambush from concealed snow-pits with barbed bone spears, vanishing completely during whiteouts. They ransom stranded travelers for firewood and warmth. Their snow earths melt away each spring.
Combat: 4/1/4/3, cycle (whiteout-lure behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: white-furred maiden with bone spear and glass-charms, tail huge against cold, breath frosting. Ice-blue eyes. States: snow-pit ambush crouch / spear-rush whiteout vanish / thaw-mired stagger / snow-cave retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young snow-pup / scarred hunter / grey reader / old den-mother.

## 78. ENY_FOXMAIDEN_EMBER — Ember Maiden
Charcoal-tipped ember maidens den beside steaming volcanic foothill vents where warm ash breathes continually. Their bold bands scavenge abandoned battlefield leavings completely without fear. They understand forge-fire behavior better than apprentice smiths. Smiths trade hot slag for warnings about ground tremors. They dance nightly around glowing vents. In battle they hurl handfuls of hot cinders before slashing with fire-hardened spears. They dread quenching floods above all dangers. Their ash earths glow faintly each night.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, cycle (foxfire-dance behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: ash-dark maiden with fire-hardened spear and cinder-pouch, tail tip glowing faintly, soot-streaked fur. Cracked pads. States: vent-side dance pause / cinder-hurl spear slash / doused whimper stagger / ash-burrow retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young spark / scarred scavenger / grey dancer / old ash-mother.

## 79. ENY_FOXMAIDEN_MIST — Mist Maiden
Grey-mantled mist maidens drift silently through dense wetland fogs where narrow causeways vanish completely. Their patient bands guide genuinely lost travelers onward in exchange for correctly answered riddles. They remember every drowned path across generations. Lonely boatmen hum answering tunes back through fog. They steal unattended lanterns compulsively. In battle they mislead pursuers with perfect mimicry before hamstringing stragglers from concealing fog, never standing openly. They trade safe guidance for new stories. Their fog earths flood deeply each year.
Combat: 3/1/5/3, cycle (mimicry-lure behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: grey-furred maiden with mimic voice and hamstring knife, tail low in fog, eyes luminous. Damp pelt. States: fog-drift song / mimicry hamstring rush / lantern-loss stagger / fog-swallow retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young whisper / scarred guide / grey riddler / old fog-mother.

## 80. ENY_FOXMAIDEN_MATRON — Fox Matron (band-level only)
Great-tailed matrons lead quarrelsome maiden bands, settling den disputes and remembering distant famine years. Their seasoned counsel alone decides seasonal migrations between earths. They shelter trembling cubs personally during wars and floods. Younger bands obey their subtle brush-twitch signals instantly. Curious travelers seek their famous riddle-wisdom at edges. They demand new stories as consultation price. In battle they coordinate encircling rushes through tail-signals, shielding young behind experienced fighters. Their sudden fall splits bands toward rival daughters.
Combat: 4/1/5/5, cycle (tale-command behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: imposing great-tailed elder with story-staff and many-ringed tail, presence calm and cunning, larger than daughters. Silvered muzzle. States: tail-signal command stance / coordinated encircling rush / daughter-challenge stagger / den-retreat surrender. Variants: 4F young herald / prime matron / grey rememberer / old tale-keeper.

# Humanoid Family 20 — Indian trio Rakshasa/Yaksha/Garuda (5, high-count, outside bands; Garuda name held here, Beast uses Roc), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 81. ENY_RAKSHASA_CLAW — Rakshasa Claw
Tiger-striped rakshasa bands prowl dense forest borders lying beyond the last rice terraces. Their clawed fighters revere personal strength and small cairns raised over notable kills. They learned patient ambush techniques from watching great cats hunt deer. Villagers bar their doors quickly at the first roars. They collect heavy iron bangles from fallen foes. They fear swift river crossings more than spears. In battle they rake with iron-shod claws, leaping suddenly from concealing cover. They challenge enemy champions to single combat proudly.
Combat: 6/1/3/3, always_physical.
Appearance: tiger-striped horned fighter with iron claws and bangle-armor, fanged grin, bulk overtowering humans. Loping gait. States: border-prowl crouch / leaping rake / river-balked stagger / cairn-retreat surrender. Variants: 2M young claw / scarred reaver; 2F hard claw-sister / older keeper.

## 82. ENY_RAKSHASA_DANCER — Rakshasa Dancer
Masked dancer rakshasas perform terrifying swirling war-dances immediately before every nighttime raid. Their watching bands fight noticeably emboldened by pounding shield-skin rhythms. They remember ancient massacre sites and revisit them yearly. They demand strong wine before performing. In battle they whirl gracefully with twin short swords, dazzling foes before cutting deeply. They falter badly without supporting drums. Their leering masks terrify village children for miles. Captured dancers laugh defiantly until ransomed.
Combat: 5/1/4/3, always_physical.
Appearance: masked whirling fighter with twin swords and drum-shield, striped limbs blurring, bells jingling. States: war-dance spin / dazzling twin-cut / drum-loss falter stagger / masked kneeling surrender. Variants: 2M young dancer / scarred performer; 2F hard dancer / older drummer.

## 83. ENY_YAKSHA_GUARD — Yaksha Guard
Barrel-bodied yaksha bands guard mossy grove shrines they diligently tend without claiming priesthood. Their stout fighters wield heavy knotted clubs against encroaching loggers. They adore gambling with carved bone dice. Passing travelers pay small tolls in wine. They dimly remember buried hoards from fallen regimes. In battle they stand immovable like gateposts with great clubs, holding sacred ground. They pursue detected thieves relentlessly for miles. Their dice rolls decide nightly watches fairly.
Combat: 6/1/2/3, always_physical.
Appearance: barrel-bodied warden with great club and dice-pouch, belly vast, stance rooted like a stump. Bearded. States: shrine-guard stance / club-rooted smash / grove-fire panic stagger / seated dice-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young guard / mossy elder; 2F hard guard / older keeper.

## 84. ENY_YAKSHA_TRADER — Yaksha Trader
Jovial trader yakshas haul heavy forest goods between isolated bands upon enormously broad backs. Their slow caravans peacefully bridge even actively feuding neighboring troops. They love prolonged bargaining above all pleasures. They readily extend credit to hungry strangers. In battle they defend precious packs with thick cudgels, retreating steadily with goods intact. They prefer ransoming captives rather than killing outright. Their knotted-cord ledgers record every debt faithfully.
Combat: 4/1/4/4, always_physical.
Appearance: broad-backed carrier with cudgel and cord-ledger bundles, smiling tusks, pack towering overhead. States: pack-braced stand / cudgel-swing retreat / pack-cut stagger / seated offering goods surrender. Variants: 2M young carrier / fat broker; 2F hard carrier / older money-keeper.

## 85. ENY_GARUDA_SCOUT — Garuda Scout
Eagle-winged garuda scouts patrol soaring thermal heights high above sprawling lowland forests. Their incredibly keen sight accurately counts marching caravans many miles away. They revere open sky above all earthly things. They despise coiling Naga with ancient instinctive rivalry, clashing violently on sight. They trade molted flight feathers for precious salt. They fear enclosed caves more than arrows. In battle they dive screaming with talon-spears, never landing among leveled spears. They occasionally rescue stranded travelers from cliffs.
Combat: 5/1/4/3, always_physical.
Appearance: eagle-winged tall scout with talon-spear and salt-pouch, beaked face proud, wingspan shadowing humans. Golden eyes. States: thermal-soaring circle / screaming dive spear / grounded wing-drag stagger / eyrie-retreat surrender. Variants: 2M young flyer / scarred watcher; 2F swift sister / older sky-keeper.

# Humanoid Family 21 — Kobolds (5, high-count, small scavengers, separate from lizard stalkers), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 86. ENY_KOBOLD_SCRAPPER — Kobold Scrapper
Small scavenger bands infest stinking refuse pits clustered outside busy walled settlements. Their yapping packs drag fallen offal away promptly at dusk. They worship shiny scrap metal with hoarding reverence. They fear large housecats far more than human guards. In battle they swarm victims with sharpened spoons and awls, overwhelming through sheer numbers. They scatter squealing immediately at presented torches. Their reeking pits betray every nearby lair. Starved winter months drive them desperately indoors.
Combat: 2/1/4/1, always_physical.
Appearance: rat-sized dog-like scavenger with spoon-shiv and scrap satchel, standing knee-high to humans, tail wagging nervously. Mangy fur. States: pit-lurk crouch / yapping swarm stab / torch-panic scatter stagger / belly-up whimpering surrender. Variants: 2M young yapper / scarred snatcher; 2F quick dam / older hoarder.

## 87. ENY_KOBOLD_TRAPPER — Kobold Trapper
Clever wire-layers defend narrow warren mouths with strung bells and vicious foot snares. Their timid bands trade early warnings to stronger neighbors for food scraps. They learn trap-craft from watching human hunters. They hate burrowing badgers with lasting passion. In battle they ring clanging alarms before stoning invaders from low tunnels, never facing anyone openly. Their tangling wires catch friends as often as foes. Sudden floods drown entire warrens helplessly.
Combat: 2/1/5/2, always_physical.
Appearance: wire-laden small setter with bell-strings and stone-pouch, ears huge, eyes bright. States: mouth-crouch listen / alarm-ring stone-hail / wire-tangled stagger / bell-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young ringer / grey layer; 2F quick setter / older listener.

## 88. ENY_KOBOLD_HOUNDMASTER — Kobold Houndmaster
Mangy houndmasters lead ragged packs of half-wild curs through refuse-choked alleys. Their scrawny bands hunt teeming rats for every meal. They share all meat evenly with their dogs. In battle they loose snarling dogs first before jabbing with short spears. Fallen dogs rout handlers instantly. Their makeshift kennels move with each eviction. Fresh meat alone sustains doubtful loyalty.
Combat: 3/1/3/2 (handler+dogs), always_physical.
Appearance: cur-master with leash-tangles and short spear, curs snapping around knees, standing small among dogs. States: pack-release stance / spear-jab behind dogs / dog-loss wail stagger / leash-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young caller / scarred master; 2F hard caller / older breeder.

## 89. ENY_KOBOLD_TINKER — Kobold Tinker
Scrap-tinkers mend discarded broken tools with twisted wire and muttered prayers. Their hopeful bands haunt busy smithy yards long after closing hours. They love salvaged hinges above silver coins. In battle they swing light hammers weakly while attempting absurd repairs mid-fight. Their rattling carts hold unsorted junk piles everywhere. Cold rain rusts their entire stock overnight. A working hinge delights them beyond food. Smiths tolerate them for sweeping floors.
Combat: 2/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: hinge-laden mender with hammer and wire-spools, apron trailing ground, eyes hopeful. States: cart-lean offer / hammer-tap swing / cart-topple stagger / junk-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young fixer / grey saver; 2F quick mender / older keeper.

## 90. ENY_KOBOLD_CHIEF — Kobold Chief (band-level only)
Yapping chiefs hold crowded warrens through sheer loudness of voice. Their constant boasts rally timid packs toward risky raids. They claim polished old boots as dented crowns. In battle they screech orders while waving bent swords from atop trash thrones, dueling rival yappers personally. Their sudden fall scatters packs into hiding instantly. Rival chiefs absorb leaderless survivors quickly. A consistently full midden keeps uneasy peace. Hungry challengers test them nightly without mercy.
Combat: 3/1/3/4, always_physical.
Appearance: boot-crowned small chief with bent sword and trash throne, voice shrill, chest puffed. States: throne-stand screech / sword-wave duel / throne-topple stagger / boot-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young yapper / scarred holder; 2F hard dam-chief / older matron.

Spotted hyena-folk bands lope tirelessly across dry scrub where old carcasses draw them for miles. Their unsettling cackling calls disturb every night camp within earshot. They learned efficient scavenging from watching circling vultures patiently. Drovers dread their approaching giggles more than wolves. They collect cracked marrow bones obsessively. Massive bonebreaker gnolls crush steaming marrow bones with heavy stone mauls. Their hungry bands camp stubbornly at fresh kill sites for days. They remember hidden waterholes across drought years. They trade cured hides for iron spearheads. In battle they smash raised shields with pulverizing mauls, opening holes for packmates. They gorge themselves mid-fight when blood flows. Lean hunter gnolls run down swift gazelles across shimmering open flats daily. Their disciplined bands coordinate exhausting relay pursuits for many hours. They hamstring fallen stragglers ruthlessly without hesitation. Their legendary endurance outlasts cavalry horses easily. Sharp thorn fields lame them quickly every season. A lost scent ends hunts immediately.

## 94. ENY_GNOLL_SPEAKER — Gnoll Speaker (no true magic, tricks only)
Cackling speaker gnolls conduct rattling rites over carefully cracked skull piles. Their supposed power is infectious laughter and acrid rot-smoke, never true magic. Bands fight visibly emboldened beneath howling. In battle they howl quavering calls before striking with knobbed staves. Their sudden fall quiets packs instantly. Their masks are hollowed hyena skulls. Cold rain stills their rites completely.

Dominant packlord gnolls hold many quarreling families through repeated victories. Their chilling giggles silence disputes instantly. Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.

## 103. ENY_BOARHEAD_SPEAKER — Boar Speaker (no true magic)
Mud-painted speakers grunt winding prophecies over freshly found truffle beds. Their coarse authority rests on scent-reading and tremendous squeals, never sorcery. Subordinate bands follow their dripping snouts toward food unfailingly. They anoint chosen champions with sacred loam. In battle they squeal piercing charges then jab with ash staves, rallying young fighters shrilly. Their sudden fall quiets grunting herds instantly. Their cracking mud-masks bake hard in summer sun.
Combat: 3/1/4/4, cycle (squeal-then-jab, mundane).
Appearance: mud-masked grunter with prophecy staff and truffle-pouch, paint cracked, breath hot. States: sniffing prophecy stance / squeal-charge staff jab / mask-washed stagger / mud-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young sniffer / grey grunt; 2F hard sow-speaker / older dam.

## 104. ENY_BOARHEAD_RAIDER — Boar Raider
Armored raiders ride enormous feral hogs, crashing straight through thorn hedges. Their thundering cavalry terrifies walled villages for miles. They shoe their slavering mounts with hammered iron. Intimidated farriers dread their visits yearly. In battle they ride down wavering lines with ash lances, wheeling expertly for second passes. Dismounted they flounder helplessly in mud. Their ravenous hogs devour absolutely anything. Lamed mounts strand riders permanently.
Combat: 5/1/3/3 (rider+mount), always_physical.
Appearance: lance-armed rider atop a feral war-hog, armor patched, tusks filed, both snorting. States: hedge-pawing trot / crashing lance pass / unseated wallow stagger / mount-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young rider / scarred chief; 2F hard rider / older breeder.

## 105. ENY_BOARHEAD_CHIEF — Boar Chief (band-level only)
Enormous chiefs hold grunting sounders together through sheer weight and insatiable appetite. Their nightly feasts decide the next dawn’s raids. Young challengers test their tusks constantly. In battle they duel enemy champions personally with great cleavers while sounders clash around. Their sudden death splits sounders into feuding splinters. Their steaming middens grow larger each year. A consistently full trough keeps fragile peace.
Combat: 7/1/2/4, always_physical.
Appearance: enormous tusked chief with great cleaver and trough-throne, bulk dwarfing kin, scars layered. States: trough-stand challenge / great-cleaver duel / tusk-cracked stagger / trough-yielding surrender. Variants: 2M young challenger / scarred holder; 2F hard matron / older sow-queen.

# Humanoid Family 25 — Lizard marsh stalkers (5, high-count, separate from kobolds; distinct from Naga and croc-folk), Phase 1, no JSON — EXPANDED full 8x8+
Rule: city-free, every sketch sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 106. ENY_LIZARD_STALKER — Marsh Stalker
Scaled marsh stalkers glide silently through reed-channels where dark water hides every approach. Their patient bands ambush thirsty drinking herds at purple dusk. They learned statue-stillness from basking crocodiles over generations. Nervous fisherfolk trade fresh fish for warning clicks. They collect smooth sun-warmed stones compulsively. They fear sudden cold snaps greatly. In battle they erupt roaring with barbed spears, dragging screaming victims into deep water. They abandon hunts quickly against prepared fire.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: scaled green stalker with barbed spear and stone-pouch, tail balancing, eyes lidless, human-tall and low. States: channel-float ambush / erupting spear-drag / cold-stiffened stagger / nest-retreat surrender. Variants: 2M young floater / scarred patient; 2F nesting dam / older keeper.

## 107. ENY_LIZARD_DARTER — Marsh Darter
Slim darters sprint tirelessly across sucking mudflats on two balancing legs, herding silver fish into woven nets. Their coordinated bands drive schools with waving tail-flags. They race each other for sport at dawn. They trade salted roe to villagers for iron. In battle they encircle victims with whistling light javelins, never standing to trade blows. They tire visibly after long pursuits. Their bright flags signal every turn. Mired black mud traps them helplessly.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: slim two-legged darter with light javelins and tail-flag, lean and quick, smaller than humans. States: mudflat sprint circle / encircling javelin rain / mired flail stagger / flag-lowered surrender. Variants: 2M young runner / grey driver; 2F swift dam / older netter.

## 108. ENY_LIZARD_DIVER — Marsh Diver
Deep divers hold their breath for long dark minutes beneath black still water. Their silent bands pry blue mussels and lost iron from sunken wrecks. They navigate lightless depths purely by touch. Grateful divers trade rare salvage for lamp oil. They fear barbed hooks above all. In battle they capsize rocking skiffs from below, drowning panicked swimmers mercilessly. They cannot fight effectively ashore. Their sunken caches glitter with old coins.
Combat: 4/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: broad-chested diver with salvage net and bone knife, gill-slits fluttering, eyes large. States: black-water hover / capsize-drag drown / beached gasp stagger / cache-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young diver / grey lug; 2F hard diver / older keeper.

## 109. ENY_LIZARD_SUNSEER — Sunseer (no true magic)
Basking sunseers read coming weather in glossy scale-sheen and towering cloud color. Their dozing counsel picks seasonal migrations for the bands. Basking bands coil respectfully around their warm rocks. They loudly claim sun-favor, though power is observation only. They anoint departing scouts with sacred mud. In battle they chant rasping hymns then jab with fire-hardened staves, rallying young fighters. Their sudden fall scatters basking bands instantly. Their sacred basking rocks draw pilgrims.
Combat: 3/1/5/4, cycle (chant-then-jab, mundane).
Appearance: sun-bleached basker with staff and mud-bowl, scales pale, posture sprawled. States: rock-bask chant / staff-jab rally / cloud-covered falter stagger / mud-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young basker / grey reader; 2F warm dam / older seer.

## 110. ENY_LIZARD_MIRELORD — Mirelord (band-level only)
Scarred mirelord lizards hold flooded territories through repeated crushing victories. Their deep bellows silence disputes among younger stalkers instantly. Subordinate bands follow their deepest croaks obediently. In battle they crush challengers personally with massive jaws and sweeping tails, dueling champions while bands clash. Their sudden death splits territories into warring reaches. Their sinking mud-thrones require yearly rebuilding. A consistently full marsh keeps fragile peace.
Combat: 7/1/2/4, always_physical.
Appearance: massive scarred stalker with jaw-scars and mud-throne mound, bulk dwarfing kin, bellow shaking reeds. States: bellow-challenge stance / jaw-tail crush duel / jaw-wired stagger / throne-yielding surrender. Variants: 2M young challenger / scarred lord; 2F hard lady / older dam.

# Humanoid Family 26 — Satyrs (5, high-count, lowland raiders; distinct from Aegipane herders), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 111. ENY_SATYR_PIPER — Satyr Piper
Wine-flushed pipers lead reeling midnight dances in moonlit lowland groves beyond the last vineyards. Their capering bands trample young crops while chasing alluring rumors that never materialize. They learned lilting reed-tunes from highland cousins but play them faster and far drunker. Worried villagers lock cellar doors and daughters alike at dusk. They trade lewd dances eagerly for strong drink. They fear consecrated iron bells above all weapons. In battle they caper dartingly with short leaf-blades, dodging nimbly rather than parrying solidly. They rout squealing the moment sober fear takes hold.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: wine-flushed goat-legged piper with short sword and reed-pipes, wreath askew, capering gait. States: dance-circle caper / dodging sword-flourish / sober-clumsy stagger / pipe-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young dancer / grey piper; 2F hard dancer / older caller.

## 112. ENY_SATYR_RIPPER — Satyr Ripper
Briar-horned ripper satyrs tear lowland vineyard trellises apart for drunken sport. Their rowdy bands raid orchards across entire valleys each autumn. They hate every fence, wall, and locked gate. In battle they gore with sharp horns before slashing with looted cleavers. They pursue fleeing victims far beyond sense. Their horns splinter against iron shields. Sharp pruning hooks deter them effectively always.
Combat: 5/1/2/3, always_physical.
Appearance: briar-crowned ripper with looted cleaver and torn trellis-wood, horns sharp, eyes wild. States: trellis-tearing charge / gore-cleaver frenzy / horn-splintered stagger / trellis-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young tearer / scarred ripper; 2F hard ripper / older dam.

## 113. ENY_SATYR_SCOUT — Satyr Scout
Fleet-footed scout satyrs range far ahead of lumbering raiding parties through dry scrub. Their curious bands map every patrol route and vineyard guard post. They love carrying juicy gossip between distant groves. In battle they skirmish relentlessly with light javelins, never standing to trade blows. They report movements promptly to waiting ambushers. Their bounding speed outruns most hunting dogs. Well-placed snares catch them regularly at trails.
Combat: 3/1/5/2, always_physical.
Appearance: fleet scout with light javelins and gossip-scroll, legs springy, ears pricked. States: scrub-darting scout / skirmishing javelin rain / snared tumble stagger / scroll-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young runner / grey whisperer; 2F swift lass / older listener.

## 114. ENY_SATYR_BREWER — Satyr Brewer
Drunken brewer satyrs ferment mounds of stolen orchard fruit inside hollow fallen logs. Their possessive bands guard bubbling mash with jealous fury. They trade finished wine for iron weapons. They remember great vintages across decades. In battle they hurl pots of burning mash before brawling with heavy clubs, emboldened deeply by drink. They collapse weeping when suddenly sober. Their leaking logs betray camps by smell.
Combat: 4/1/3/2, cycle (mash-then-brawl, mundane).
Appearance: wine-soaked brewer with mash-pot bandolier and heavy club, staggering gait, stained muzzle. States: mash-stirring guard / burning-pot hurl brawl / sober-collapse stagger / wine-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young masher / bloated master; 2F hard brewer / older keeper.

## 115. ENY_SATYR_REVELMASTER — Revelmaster (band-level only)
Horn-crowned revelmaster satyrs command entire festival-raids with uplifted pipes and brimming cups. Their slurred toasts launch assaults on schedule. Rowdy bands obey their drunken eloquence completely. They wed no one and own nothing. In battle they toast loudly before leading horn-lowered charges personally, dueling enemy leaders. Their sudden fall sobers bands instantly. Their massive cups serve as heirlooms.
Combat: 5/1/4/4, always_physical.
Appearance: crowned reveler with great cup and pipe-scepter, presence magnetic, bulk flushed. States: cup-raised toast / horn-lowered charge duel / cup-shattered stagger / cup-offering surrender. Variants: 2M young toaster / scarred master; 2F hard mistress / older caller.

# Humanoid Family 27 — Harpy harriers (5, high-count, female-led snatcher-raiders; distinct from Sphinx singers and Siren lurers), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words (fixed versions). Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 116. ENY_HARPY_SCREECHER — Harpy Screecher
Carrion-voiced screecher flocks circle distant battlefields patiently waiting for the dying to weaken. Their hungry sisters strip abandoned corpses completely within only a few minutes. They learned to trail marching armies across many successive campaigns. They trade battlefield news to jackals for choice offal shares. They dread concentrated volleys from massed archers. In battle they deafen victims with combined shrieks before diving with extended talons. They abandon assaults immediately against prepared fire. Their cliff nests accumulate stinking trophies over many seasons.
Combat: 4/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: Vulture-winged harrier carries talons and a corpse-strip satchel, voice rasping harshly, eyes constantly hungry. Ragged plumage smells of old blood. States: battlefield-circling waiting posture / shrieking talon-diving assault / arrow-riddled tumbling stagger / nest-retreat cowering surrender. Variants: 1M flock-guard husband; 3F young screecher / prime stripper / grey caller (female-led flocks).

## 117. ENY_HARPY_SNATCHER — Harpy Snatcher
Lamb-stealing snatcher flocks haunt windswept high pastures where flocks graze completely unwatched. Their swift sisters lift bleating lambs whole into cloudy skies. Shepherds fire warning shots into the air nightly. They trade stolen wool to lowland weavers for dried fish. They compulsively collect shiny brass buckles. In battle they snatch chosen victims skyward using harness-straps, dropping those who resist violently. They carefully avoid bristling spear-hedges. Their remote eyries accumulate glittering loot piles.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: Broad-winged snatcher carries harness-straps and a wool-sack, talons massive and scarred, lift tremendously powerful. Shoulder muscles bulge. States: pasture-circling stalking flight / snatch-lift dropping assault / spear-hedged wing-tearing stagger / eyrie-retreat hasty surrender. Variants: 1M guard; 3F young lifter / prime carrier / grey watcher.

## 118. ENY_HARPY_PUNISHER — Harpy Punisher
Oath-keeping punisher flocks torment thieves who desecrate lonely cliff shrines. Their stern sisters enforce ancient boundary customs without mercy. They remember every insult across long decades. They demand heavy weregild payments in livestock. In battle they scourge chosen leaders with knotted cords, ignoring common fighters. They spare those who pay promptly. Their weighted cords cut deeply through leather. Bribed punishers lie unconvincingly and are cast out.
Combat: 4/1/5/3, always_physical.
Appearance: Stern cord-bearing harrier carries knotted scourges and shrine-tokens, gaze coldly judging, wings folded precisely. Beak polished. States: shrine-perch judging vigil / scourging leader-striking assault / bribe-exposed shame stagger / shrine-retreat sullen surrender. Variants: 1M guard; 3F young scourge / prime judge / grey rememberer.

## 119. ENY_HARPY_STORMRIDER — Harpy Stormrider
Gale-riding stormrider flocks follow towering thunderheads across exposed high plateaus. Their bold sisters hunt clearly in flickering lightning light. They read sudden pressure drops with perfect accuracy. Worried herders watch their flight for storm warnings. They nest exclusively among sharp stormglass spires. In battle they dive screaming through driving rain with slate shards, scattering shield lines. They cannot sustain flight in completely calm air. Their glittering nests overlook entire valleys.
Combat: 5/1/3/2, always_physical.
Appearance: Storm-grey rider carries a slate-shard satchel, wings enormously broad, hail scars across shoulders. Eyes electric. States: thunderhead-circling soaring patrol / rain-diving shard-scattering assault / becalmed flopping helpless stagger / nest-retreat windswept surrender. Variants: 1M guard; 3F young rider / prime diver / grey reader.

## 120. ENY_HARPY_MATRIARCH — Harpy Matriarch (band-level only)
Iron-beaked matriarchs command entire cliff colonies through screaming decrees. Their seasoned counsel alone determines seasonal migrations. Subordinate flocks obey their gestures instantly. They shelter shivering fledglings personally during violent storms. They demand regular tribute in healthy livestock. In battle they coordinate devastating multi-flock dives using signal banner-wings, shielding young behind veterans. Their sudden fall splits colonies toward rival daughters. Their heavy beaks grey visibly first.
Combat: 5/1/4/5, always_physical.
Appearance: Imposing iron-beaked elder carries banner-wings and a tribute-pile, presence overwhelmingly dominating, size greater than daughters. Voice shattering. States: decree-screaming commanding posture / coordinated multi-diving assault / beak-cracked reeling stagger / colony-yielding bitter surrender. Variants: 1M consort-guard; 3F young herald / prime commander / grey matriarch.

Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 121. ENY_HAG_OUTCAST — Hag Outcast
Wart-skinned outcast hags dwell alone in leaking hovels beyond the last village wells. Their crooked fingers gather bitter herbs that honest wives refuse to touch. They remember slights across many long decades without forgiving. Villagers blame them loudly for every stillbirth and failed crop. They trade dubious charms for bread crusts. They keep one-eyed cats as sole companions. In battle they curse loudly while striking with iron ladles, fighting with desperate strength. They surrender quickly when grandchildren intervene tearfully.

## 122. ENY_HAG_MIRE — Mire Hag
Bog-dwelling mire hags nest upon floating peat islands inside trackless fens. Their bloated fingers weave eel-traps from human hair scavenged downstream. They lure travelers nightly with false lantern lights over water. Drained marsh crews fear the sucking pools surrounding each island. They collect drowned boots as trophies beside their dripping beds. They understand tides better than most experienced river pilots. Their hooked poles drag victims into black water during battle. They release prisoners who correctly solve their patient riddles.
Combat: 4/3/5/2, cycle (mire curses flagged proposal per magic doctrine; never final).
Appearance: Bloated peat-stained elder carries a hooked pole and lantern-cage, hair dripping, breath marsh-gas foul. Skin green-grey. Variants: 4F young bog-wife / prime lure / grey weaver / old peat-mother.

## 123. ENY_LESHY_WARDEN — Leshy Warden (fixed)
Moss-bearded forest wardens patrol ancient timber stands they solemnly claim to protect. Their towering frames shift subtly among dark trunks, confusing even seasoned woodsmen completely. They punish greedy poachers by leading them in hopeless circles for days. Charcoal-burners leave sweet honey offerings respectfully at mossy boundary stumps. They marry no one and accumulate no property anywhere. They understand roaming wolf packs intimately across many seasons. In battle they ambush intruders with heavy root-clubs from living cover, vanishing silently between trees instantly. They release captives freely who willingly replant young saplings afterward.

Appearance: Moss-bearded towering guardian carries a heavy root-club and honey-gourd, bark-like skin creaking audibly, eyes green-gold and watchful. Height seems variable within shifting forest light always.
States: trunk-merged silent vigil / root-club ambushing assault / fire-menaced roaring stagger / sapling-planting releasing surrender. Variants: 2M old warden / grey misleader; 2F hard wife / older keeper.

## 124. ENY_LESHY_HERDER — Leshy Herder (fixed)
Mushroom-crowned herders drive clicking beetle herds and frog swarms through damp autumn woods. Their shrill piping calls gather every creeping thing for many miles around. They trade glowing shelf fungi to travelers for precious iron nails. Unwary travelers follow their drifting lights helplessly astray at night. They sleep curled inside spacious hollow oaks during daylight hours. They count tree rings meticulously every single spring. In battle they overwhelm foes with swarming bites directed by pipe-tunes, avoiding direct blows personally always. They scatter frantically whenever their fragile pipes break suddenly.

States: hollow-oak piping stance / swarm-directing overwhelming assault / pipe-snapped panicked stagger / spore-scattering pleading surrender. Variants: 2M young piper / grey caller; 2F piping sister / older brood-keeper.

In battle they coordinate multi-band drownings with compelling sung commands, shielding young behind reeds. Their sudden death frees rivers for many years. Rival queens absorb leaderless bands quickly. A flower-full summer keeps fragile peace.
Combat: 4/1/5/5, cycle (sung-command behavior-magic flagged intuitive proposal per magic doctrine; never cast).
Appearance: Willow-crowned imposing queen carries a wedding candle and drowned veil, presence sorrowful and commanding, larger than daughters. River pearls strung. States: procession-leading floating command / coordinated drowning sung assault / veil-torn wailing stagger / tribute-accepting yielding surrender. Variants: 4F young herald / prime queen / grey rememberer / old river-mother.

## 135. ENY_JOTNAR_FROST — Frost Jotunn
Rime-bearded frost jotnar stride through blinding upland snowfields far above treelines. Their massive bands hunt white bears across cracking glaciers. They carve shelters from packed snow effortlessly. Highland herders sacrifice goats at boundary cairns nervously. They collect mammoth ivory avidly. They despise thaw seasons. In battle they hurl ice boulders with terrible accuracy, shattering shield walls instantly. They pursue downhill only during deep winter.
Combat: 8/1/2/3, always_physical.
Appearance: Rime-bearded towering giant carries ice boulders and a walrus-hide cloak, breath freezing visibly, height triple human stature. Blue skin. States: snowfield-striding patrol stance / ice-boulder hurling assault / thaw-weakened sweating stagger / ivory-offering grudging surrender. Variants: 2M young strider / grey rimebeard; 2F hard icewife / older keeper.

## 136. ENY_JOTNAR_CLIFF — Cliff Jotunn
Lichen-covered cliff jotnar perch patiently upon sheer highland crags overlooking narrow passes. Their watchful bands drop heavy rocks on slow toll caravans. They understand mountain echoes perfectly. They demand fat goat tolls. In battle they trigger roaring rockslides with iron levers before throwing tors by hand. They never descend willingly to valleys. Their lofty crag nests shelter stolen eaglets as pets. Wary climbers avoid their long shadows.
Combat: 7/1/3/3, always_physical.
Appearance: Lichen-covered sitter carries lever-poles and a tor-pile, skin grey-green, legs dangling over drops. Massive hands. States: crag-perch silent vigil / rockslide-triggering throwing assault / valley-lured clumsy stagger / toll-accepting rumbling surrender. Variants: 2M young dropper / grey watcher; 2F hard cragwife / older keeper.

## 137. ENY_JOTNAR_FORGE — Forge Jotunn
Soot-blackened forge jotnar tend roaring geothermal vents inside volcanic foothill caves. Their skilled bands hammer glowing bog-iron into crude blades. They understand molten stone-melt intuitively. They trade finished blades for fresh meat. In battle they swing white-hot ingots with giant tongs, scalding through steel armor. They fear sudden quenching pools greatly. Their towering slag heaps grow higher yearly. Jealous smiths spy on them constantly.
Combat: 7/1/2/3, always_physical.
Appearance: Soot-blackened smith giant carries white-hot ingots with massive tongs, apron of sewn hides, arms corded hugely. Heat shimmering. States: vent-tending hammering stance / ingot-swinging scalding assault / quenched hissing stagger / meat-accepting grunting surrender. Variants: 2M young hammer / sooty master; 2F hard smith / older keeper.

## 138. ENY_JOTNAR_SEER — Jotunn Seer (observation only, no true prophecy)
One-eyed seer jotnar read approaching weather accurately in steaming entrails and towering cloud formations. Their cryptic counsel alone guides seasonal migrations between high pastures. Grumbling bands obey their pronouncements grudgingly but consistently. They demand endless ale as consultation payment. In battle they chant confusing verses before striking with gnarled staves, rallying young fighters strongly. Their sudden fall disheartens entire bands immediately. Their single remaining eyes misjudge depth badly.
Combat: 5/1/5/4, cycle (chant-then-strike, mundane).
Appearance: One-eyed hooded elder carries a gnarled staff and entrail-bowl, empty socket scarred, voice low and rumbling. Ravens perched. States: entrail-reading squinting vigil / chanting staff-striking assault / eye-struck howling stagger / ale-accepting muttering surrender. Variants: 2M old one-eye / grey raven-friend; 2F hard seeress / older keeper.

## 139. ENY_JOTNAR_JARL — Jotunn Jarl (band-level only)
Gold-ringed jarl jotnar rule scattered mountain holds through lavish feasting and ritual arm-wrestling contests. Their booming laughter settles bloody disputes among rival families instantly. Subordinate bands deliver prime tribute meats regularly. They wed strategically to bind feuding valleys. In battle they duel enemy champions personally with ancient pattern-welded swords, inspiring watching kin greatly. Their sudden death splits holds toward rival sons immediately. Their heavy rings record every victory faithfully.
Combat: 8/1/3/5, always_physical.
Appearance: Gold-ringed massive chief carries an ancient sword and mead-horn, rings gleaming, beard braided with gold. Presence regal. States: hall-feasting laughing command / champion-dueling inspiring assault / sword-sundered kneeling stagger / ring-yielding honorable surrender. Variants: 2M young heir / scarred jarl; 2F hard lady / older queen.

# Humanoid Families 33-36 — Duergar (2) + Fomorians (2) + Selkie (2, fixed) + Sidhe (2, fixed), Phase 1, no JSON
Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 140. ENY_DUERGAR_GREY — Grey Duergar
Ash-grey duergar bands toil endlessly in lightless quarry depths far below exile dwarves. Their sullen fighters wield heavy mattocks against everything living encountered. They remember imagined slights bitterly for many decades. They enslave stray kobolds ruthlessly without hesitation. In battle they fight silently in tight files with short spears, never routing while leaders stand firm. They poison village wells spitefully at night. Their grey skin camouflages perfectly in ash. Bright sunlight sickens them swiftly always.
Combat: 5/2/3/4, cycle (rune-craft flagged proposal per magic doctrine; never final).
Appearance: Ash-grey low fighter carries a mattock-spear and slave-chain, eyes red-rimmed, beard sparse and lank. Sunken cheeks. States: lightless-file silent advance / spear-thrust holding assault / leader-fallen routing stagger / chain-offering sullen surrender. Variants: 2M young drudge / grey taskmaster; 2F hard driver / older keeper.

## 141. ENY_DUERGAR_TASKMASTER — Duergar Taskmaster (band-level only)
Scar-lashed taskmasters drive shuffling mixed slave gangs with singing whips and tallies. Their feared bands strip productive mines completely bare within seasons. They count every lash meticulously in ledgers. They sell surplus slaves to deep buyers. In battle they flog reluctant allies forward while striking foes with weighted whips, inspiring cold terror. Their sudden death frees chained gangs instantly. Rival taskmasters absorb leaderless survivors quickly. A consistently full quota keeps uneasy peace.
Combat: 5/2/4/5, cycle (rune-craft flagged proposal per magic doctrine; never final).
Appearance: Scar-lashed driver carries a weighted whip and tally-board, voice cracking like stone, bulk squat and menacing. Branded hands. States: lash-cracking driving command / whip-striking terrifying assault / tally-smashed bewildered stagger / whip-yielding bitter surrender. Variants: 2M young lasher / scarred master; 2F hard mistress / older counter.

## 142. ENY_FOMORIAN_BRUTE — Fomorian Brute
Single-eyed brute fomorians wade ashore through crashing surf at bleak dawns. Their twisted frames combine human limbs with mismatched goat heads. They raid coastal hamlets for cattle before morning boats depart. Fisherfolk bar their doors whenever the giants begin bellowing. They demand milk tribute from frightened families near shore. Driftwood clubs sweep broadly while their horns gore opponents. Fire drives them backward through surf toward waiting water. They retreat seaward quickly when flames catch their salt-crusted hides.
Combat: 7/1/2/2, always_physical.
Appearance: Twisted goat-headed giant carries a driftwood club and tide-pool armor, single eye glaring, limbs mismatched and powerful. Salt-crusted. States: surf-wading bellowing advance / club-sweep goring assault / fire-driven seaward stagger / tribute-accepting snorting surrender. Variants: 2M young wader / scarred raider; 2F hard sea-wife / older keeper.

## 143. ENY_FOMORIAN_EYE — Fomorian Eye Tyrant (band-level only, no death-gaze as final)
Baleful-eyed tyrant fomorians direct raiding crews with hateful glaring stares rumored lethal falsely. Their actual power is uncannily precise rock-throwing, flagged never as final sorcery. Subordinate bands obey subtle eye-twitch commands instantly. They extort coastal villages for cattle regularly. In battle they hurl heavy tors with devastating accuracy while bellowing orders continuously, shielding young behind immense bulk. Their blinded fall routs crews instantly. Their deliberate blinking signals prepared retreats.
Combat: 7/1/4/4, always_physical.
Appearance: Massive single-eyed tyrant carries tor-stones and a command shell-horn, gaze baleful, bulk barnacled. Voice foghorn-deep. States: stare-directed silent command / tor-hurling bellowing assault / eye-struck screaming stagger / tribute-accepting yielding surrender. Variants: 2M young eye / scarred tyrant; 2F hard lady / older keeper.

## 144. ENY_SELKIE_SHORE — Shore Selkie (fixed)
Soft-eyed shore selkies shed heavy sealskins upon moonlit skerries in order to dance as humans. Their gentle bands net silver salmon peacefully alongside cooperative fisherfolk every dawn. They marry lonely sailors in many persistent old stories. They hide precious skins extremely carefully beneath tidal rocks. In battle they defend stolen skins ferociously with sharp gutting knives, fleeing transformation theft desperately. They ransom captured skins afterward with polished pearls. Their moonlit skerry dances entrance lonely watchers completely. Stolen skins enslave them utterly until returned.
Combat: 3/1/4/2, always_physical.
Appearance: Soft-eyed seal-woman carries a carefully folded sealskin and sharp gutting knife, movements liquid and graceful, hair black and sleek always. Human form appears graceful and vulnerable ashore.
States: skerry-dancing moonlit vigil / skin-defending knife assault / skin-seized despairing stagger / pearl-offering tearful surrender. Variants: 2M dark husband / grey bull; 2F young maid / grey skin-keeper.

## 145. ENY_SELKIE_DEEP — Deep Selkie (fixed)
Grey-bodied deep selkies hunt cold cod far beyond sight of familiar land. Their completely silent bands follow migrating whale pods across deep oceans. They navigate accurately by low song-currents only. They trade beachcombed amber to smiths for precious iron. In battle they ram fragile boats with immense bulk in seal form, then stab survivors with bone daggers ashore afterwards. They cannot remain landlocked for very long periods. Their remote deep skerries remain permanently unmapped by pilots. Warm shallow waters sicken them rapidly.
Combat: 4/1/3/3, always_physical.
Appearance: Grey-bodied powerful selkie carries sharp bone daggers and a heavy amber pouch, bulk solid and scarred, eyes large and dark always. Seal form appears massive beside small boats.
States: migration-following silent patrol / boat-ramming dagger assault / landlocked gasping stagger / amber-offering deep surrender. Variants: 2M old bull / scarred diver; 2F hard cow / older singer.

## 146. ENY_SIDHE_WANDERER — Sidhe Wanderer (fixed)
Silver-tongued sidhe wanderers walk lonely twilight roads between ancient grassy mounds. Their luminous bands trade riddling news gladly for shallow cream bowls. They remember solemn oaths sworn long before successor kings ruled. Frightened villagers bar heavy doors tightly during their glowing processions. They adore haunting music far above cold silver. They fear cracked iron bells above all weapons. In battle they dazzle victims with polished mirrored shields before cutting with thin swords, vanishing into cold mists afterwards completely. They ransom captured travelers afterward in exchange for new songs.
Combat: 4/4/5/3, cycle (court glamour flagged proposal per magic doctrine applicable-user; never final).
Appearance: Luminous pale wanderer carries a polished mirrored shield and slender thin sword, beauty profoundly unsettling, movements impossibly graceful always. Long hair shines brightly like captured moonlight constantly.
States: twilight-road procession vigil / dazzling sword-cutting assault / bell-rung scattering stagger / song-accepting bowing surrender. Variants: 2M pale rider / grey whisperer; 2F bright lady / older keeper.

## 147. ENY_SIDHE_MOUND — Sidhe Mound Keeper (band-level only, fixed)
Moss-crowned mound keepers guard rounded green hill doors opening only on feast nights. Their solemn bands collect regular tolls of fresh milk and wild honey. They remember long-buried kings beneath every hill. They punish oath-breakers cruelly with eternally losing forest ways. In battle they mislead intruders with floating lantern lights before spearing from dark mounds, never pursuing beyond grassy hills. Their heavy doors grind shut exactly at dawn. Their golden honey stores attract hungry bears constantly. A deliberately drained mound kills them very slowly.
Combat: 4/4/4/4, cycle (mound glamour flagged proposal per magic doctrine; never final).
Appearance: Moss-crowned solemn keeper carries a tall lantern-pole and heavy hill-key, presence deeply ancient, eyes star-filled and distant. Heavy green cloak drags damply behind.
States: hill-door guarding vigil / lantern-misleading spearing assault / dawn-caught blinking stagger / honey-accepting yielding surrender. Variants: 2M old doorkeeper / grey toller; 2F stern lady / older keeper.

Rule: city-free, every sentence 8+ words. Taxonomy Humanoid.

## 148. ENY_MINOTAUR_MAZE — Maze Minotaur
Horned maze minotaurs haunt crumbling quarry labyrinths beyond patrolled roads. Their brooding bands memorize every twisting passage without error. They challenge lost intruders to ritual single combat underground. Quarry crews leave meat offerings near familiar tunnel mouths. They collect every labyrinth map with obsessive, suspicious care. Open plains leave them exposed, frightened, and deeply confused. Massive horns gore opponents before stone mauls crush them. They spare defeated challengers who kneel immediately and surrender.

## 149. ENY_MINOTAUR_FORGE — Forge Minotaur
Soot-horned forge minotaurs tend charcoal pits inside foothill caves. Their patient bands hammer crude bronze with deliberate pride. They understand bellows rhythms through long practice beside furnaces. They trade finished blades for grain from nearby farmers. White-hot bars swing from tongs and scald through shields. Quenching floods terrify them because water ruins their furnaces. Black slag heaps mark their territory beside smoking cave mouths. Their horned smiths defend every pit with unyielding strength.
Combat: 7/1/2/3, always_physical.

## 150. ENY_CENTAUR_PLAINS — Plains Centaur
Four-hooved plains centaurs roam windswept valley grasslands in small herds. Their proud bands race approaching storms for pure joy. They remember old migration routes through each changing season. Farmers trade grain for warnings about dangerous roaming threats. They despise fences that break their traditional running paths. Storm feathers collected after gales decorate their braided manes. They loose short bows while galloping beyond spear reach. Fallen foes risk merciless trampling beneath their thundering hooves.
Combat: 5/1/4/3, always_physical.

Variants: 2M young runner / scarred racer; 2F swift mare / older keeper.

## 151. ENY_CENTAUR_WOOD — Wood Centaur
Bark-armored wood centaurs patrol dense forest borders where carts move slowly. Their wary bands escort travelers after collecting reasonable tolls. They understand timber prices and watch every loaded wagon. Clear-cuts provoke their anger and lasting woodland grief. They block narrow trails with their considerable bulk. Long spears strike from behind their living wooden armor. Deep woods shelter their retreat when trails become unsafe. Their bark armor regrows each year after winter storms.
Combat: 5/1/3/4, always_physical.
Appearance: Bark-armored stocky centaur carries a long spear and toll-board, flanks scarred by branches, torso thick and weathered. Leaves tangled. States: border-trail blocking stance / bulk-shoving spear assault / clear-cut grieving stagger / toll-accepting escorting surrender. Variants: 2M young guard / mossy elder; 2F hard mare / older warden.

Single-eyed herder cyclopes drive shaggy mountain sheep across lonely windswept high pastures. Their lumbering bands whistle tunelessly through hollowed bone flutes constantly. Eel-tailed marsh lamiae coil patiently in blackwater pools tangled beneath ancient willow roots. Their whispering bands perfectly mimic lost children’s frightened cries nightly. Stone-hided hill divs squat heavily upon windswept ridges overlooking narrow caravan tracks. Their brooding bands demand regular tolls in healthy livestock and workable iron. Smoke-voiced enchanter divs bluff nervous travelers with bright colored powders and practiced ventriloquism. Their storied sorcery bends smoke and unwary minds alike, flagged proposal never final. Subordinate bands obey their drifting smoke signals promptly. They extort isolated villages mercilessly each season. Their practiced ventriloquism fools only frightened children reliably. Exposed tricksters flee howling into hills immediately.

Black-armored stalker bands skitter silently across moonlit dunes where trapped day heat lingers. Their chitinous warriors wield barbed spears alongside curling scorpion tails. Enormous broodwarden scorpion-folk guard humid nursery chambers beneath rolling dunes. Their authoritative clicking commands direct distant foragers with precision. Lean jackal-headed trackers trot tirelessly across shimmering dune seas. Their disciplined bands follow cold trails beneath shifting sand. They find lost caravans for carefully negotiated water prices. Wind shifts reveal information they read with practiced accuracy. They despise hyena-folk with intense and enduring hostility. Their tireless endurance easily outlasts horses across dry distances.
